Root cause: RF is a tree-based model invariant to monotonic feature transforms — z-score shifts/scales features but preserves relative ordering, so split thresholds adapt automatically. Prior independent verification (GPT-2 XL 0.13→0.68, OLMo 0.25→0.83) was with a scale-sensitive classifier (LR), not RF. Raw results match cross_strategy_loo_001 exactly (mean acc=0.5908). Non-temp09 impact negligible (mean Δd1v2=+0.006). confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. {
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport json\nwith open('/home/ubuntu/.claude-account-1/projects/-home-ubuntu--agent-ml-research-idea-gen-0513-2-projects-recursive-gen-depth-est--claude-sessions-agent-a493998e/319ee6eb-9f55-433e-bbdb-33ae134799b4/tool-results/mcp-agent-ml-research-read_registry-1778860020065.txt') as f:\n    data = json.load(f)\nresult = json.loads(data['result'])\nexps = result['data']\nprint('Type:', type(exps))\nif isinstance(exps, dict):\n    print('Keys:', list(exps.keys())[:5])\n    # It might be a dict of exp_id -> exp\n    items = list(exps.values()) if all(isinstance(v, dict) for v in list(exps.values())[:3]) else []\n    if items:\n        from collections import Counter\n        statuses = Counter(e.get('status','?') for e in items)\n        print('\\n=== Status Summary ===')\n        for s, c in statuses.most_common():\n            print(f'  {s}: {c}')\n        print('\\n=== Non-DONE ===')\n        for e in items:\n            if e.get('status') != 'DONE':\n                print(f\\\"  {e.get('exp_id','?')}: status={e.get('status')}, conclusion={e.get('conclusion','N/A')}\\\")\n        print('\\n=== PREPARING Details ===')\n        for e in items:\n            if e.get('status') == 'PREPARING':\n                print(f\\\"  {e.get('exp_id','?')}: hypothesis={e.get('hypothesis','N/A')[:100]}\\\")\n        done = [e for e in items if e.get('status') == 'DONE']\n        done.sort(key=lambda x: x.get('updated_at',''), reverse=True)\n        print('\\n=== Latest 5 Completed ===')\n        for e in done[:5]:\n            m = json.dumps(e.get('metrics',{}))[:200]\n            print(f\\\"  {e.get('exp_id','?')}: conclusion={e.get('conclusion')}, updated={e.get('updated_at','?')[:16]}, metrics={m}\\\")\nelif isinstance(exps, list):\n    print('Length:', len(exps))\n    print('First:', type(exps[0]) if exps else 'empty')\n\"\n",
  "description": "Parse registry data structure"
}

{
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport json\nwith open('/home/ubuntu/.claude-account-1/projects/-home-ubuntu--agent-ml-research-idea-gen-0513-2-projects-recursive-gen-depth-est--claude-sessions-agent-a493998e/319ee6eb-9f55-433e-bbdb-33ae134799b4/tool-results/mcp-agent-ml-research-read_registry-1778860020065.txt') as f:\n    data = json.load(f)\nresult = json.loads(data['result'])\nexps_list = result['data']['experiments']\nfrom collections import Counter\nstatuses = Counter(e.get('status','?') for e in exps_list)\nprint('=== Status Summary ===')\nfor s, c in statuses.most_common():\n    print(f'  {s}: {c}')\nprint(f'Total: {len(exps_list)}')\n\nprint('\\n=== Non-DONE ===')\nfor e in exps_list:\n    if e.get('status') != 'DONE':\n        print(f\\\"  {e['exp_id']}: status={e.get('status')}, conclusion={e.get('conclusion','N/A')}\\\")\n\nprint('\\n=== PREPARING Details ===')\nfor e in exps_list:\n    if e.get('status') == 'PREPARING':\n        print(f\\\"  {e['exp_id']}: hypothesis={e.get('hypothesis','N/A')[:120]}\\\")\n\ndone = [e for e in exps_list if e.get('status') == 'DONE']\ndone.sort(key=lambda x: x.get('updated_at',''), reverse=True)\nprint('\\n=== Latest 5 Completed ===')\nfor e in done[:5]:\n    m = json.dumps(e.get('metrics',{}))[:200]\n    print(f\\\"  {e['exp_id']}: conclusion={e.get('conclusion')}, updated={e.get('updated_at','?')[:16]}\\\")\n    print(f\\\"    metrics={m}\\\")\n\nprint('\\n=== Baseline ===')\nprint(json.dumps(result['data'].get('baseline',{}), indent=2)[:500])\n\"\n",

}

GPT-2 XL temp09 升 2.1pp，OLMo temp09 升 3.26pp。论文不能说\\\"strategy-agnostic\\\"，应说\\\"strategy-dependent magnitude with consistent signal presence\\\"。(2026-05-15)\\n- **OLMo temp09 是当前最强配置 (D008)**: 82.03% 4-class RF, d1v2 AUC 0.901。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Feature importance 表述 (D007)**: top feature set (p90_ppl, var_surprisal) 跨 cells 稳定，但 ranking 因模型而异。Claim 2 应说\\\"consistent feature set\\\"而非\\\"var_surprisal as single best\\\"。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Top-k 信号衰减**: Pythia top-k 69.6% vs nucleus 78.2% (-8.6pp)，截断式采样比连续概率重分配保留更弱的深度信号。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Atlas v2 EDD 差异 (D009)**: 6/7 cells EDD=3，但 Pythia topk50 EDD=1（d2v3 AUC=0.692）。\\\"小模型+受限采样→退化信号更早饱和\\\"是 Discussion 核心论点。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Atlas v2 Feature trajectory (D010)**: p90_ppl/var_surprisal/mean_surprisal 随 depth 单调递减（ρ=-1.0），rep_2gram/3gram/4gram 随 depth 单调递增。两类信号互补。(2026-05-15)\\n- **EDD 推荐阈值 T=0.75 (D011)**: T=0.75 下 7/9 维持 EDD=3，仅 topk 掉队。瓶颈统一在 d2v3 pair。(2026-05-15)\\n- **topk 跨模型一致性最高 (D012)**: ρ=0.811，确定性截断使 feature landscape 趋同，但伴随信号衰减。(2026-05-15)\\n- **p90_ppl 优于 mean_ppl 的统计根因**: 1 个样本 (mean_ppl=1.43e13) 贡献 depth-1 mean_ppl 的 94.4%。Token-level perplexity 极度重尾（kurtosis>4900）。p90_ppl 完美单调。论文应说明 percentile-based features 的统计稳健性优势。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Cross-strategy LOO temp09 d1v2 反转根因 (D014, updated 2026-05-15)**: Covariate shift 确认。temp09 depth N ≈ 其他策略 depth N+1。**⚠️ 重要修正：z-score 修复效果取决于分类器类型且实验验证与 framing 声称不符**。RF 对 z-score 完全不变（树模型 scale-invariant）。LR cross-strategy z-score 实验 (lr_zscore_cross_strategy_001) 结果：GPT-2 XL temp09 holdout d1v2 AUC 0.50→0.24（framing 声称 0.13→0.68），OLMo 0.50→0.19（framing 声称 0.25→0.83）。**z-score 对 LR temp09 holdout d1v2 AUC 无效甚至更差**，说明偏移不仅是 scale 问题。论文必须删除/修改 z-score 恢复 LR AUC 的 claim。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Cross-model LOO 3-class (plan_005 detailed)**: mean acc 55.4%，d2 recall 最低 (0.409)——d2 夹在 d1/d3 之间最难区分，符合 ordinal structure 预期。(2026-05-15)\\n- **6-class pairwise AUC 不可与 4-class 直比**: 决策边界稀释导致系统性偏低（d2v3 从 4-class 0.80 降到 0.69）。EDD 判定必须用 dedicated binary pairwise classifier。(2026-05-15)\\n- **EDD=4 (Pythia nucleus, binary pairwise 验证)**: d3v4 AUC=0.784 (>0.75 ✅), d4v5 AUC=0.683 (<0.75 ❌)。深度信号在 d4 仍可区分，d5 饱和。Binary classifier 一致优于 4-class pipeline（d1v2: 0.925 vs 0.861, d2v3: 0.823 vs 0.801），确认决策边界稀释效应。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Granularity ablation 3-class < 4-class 是结构性的，非异常 (D019)**: 3-class 排除 d0（real text），所以 accuracy 低于 4-class。含 d0 的粒度趋势：binary 99.5% > 4-class 78.3% > 6-class 62.9%，严格单调下降。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Boundary dilution 量化 (D020)**: d2v3 AUC 从 4-class 0.804 → 6-class 0.689（-14.3%），d0v1 AUC 几乎不受影响（≥0.997）。稀释效应集中在相邻深度边界。(2026-05-15)\\n- **N2 双 backbone 架构无关性 (2026-05-15)**: RoBERTa-base (59.5% ± 3.2%) 和 DeBERTa-v3-base (58.3% ± 4.7%) end-to-end finetune 结果高度一致（差 1.2pp），均大幅低于 RF 78.2%。排除了 reviewer concern \\\"也许换个 PLM 就能追上 RF\\\"。两种架构（MLM vs disentangled attention）得到相同结论 = architecture-independent finding。论文可以用 \\\"architecture-independent\\\" 措辞。\\n- **PLM 表征 vs 手工特征互补性 (2026-05-15)**: DeBERTa feature overlap analysis: Spearman ρ=-0.484 (p=0.036), top-5 feature overlap=0。PLM 擅长编码表面特征 (TTR R²=0.85, rep_2gram R²=0.82)，而 RF 依赖的 perplexity 分布统计量 (var_surprisal, p90_ppl) 在 DeBERTa 表征中 R² 为负。论文 story: hand-crafted statistical features 捕获了 PLM 无法从 raw text 学到的递归深度信号——不是\\\"选错了 PLM\\\"，而是两类方法关注文本的不同层面。\\n\\n## 约束\\n\\n- **submit_on_allocated bash 语法**: tmux 跑在 /bin/sh 下，必须用 `bash -c \\\"...\\\"` 包裹 bash-only 语法（如 source）。(2026-05-15)\\n- **sync_code 不可靠**: submit_on_allocated 的 sync_code=true 可能不同步新创建的文件。安全方案：Worker SSH 手动写入。(2026-05-15)\\n- **CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 与脚本 device 参数冲突**: submit_training_job 自动注入 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 映射物理 GPU，脚本内必须用 cuda:0（逻辑第一块卡）。若脚本硬编码物理 GPU 编号（如 cuda:1），会报 \\\"CUDA error: invalid device ordinal\\\"。多步 pipeline 中某些步骤可能在 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 生效前运行（如 shell 脚本前几步），但后续 Python 脚本一律用 cuda:0。(2026-05-15)\\n\\n## 经验教训\\n\\n- **DCOL 全系列失败 (D018)**: v1-v5 DeBERTa 数值不稳定，v6 RoBERTa 稳定但 acc=64.5% << RF 78.2%。Contrastive ordinal loss 对此任务表达能力不足。(2026-05-15)\\n- **模型文件损坏需提前验证 (2026-05-15)**: n2_roberta_finetune_001 因 pytorch_model.bin 损坏重试 2 次。下载大模型后应立即验证文件完整性（检查 filesize 或 md5），避免训练中途才发现。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Phase 2 Writing Prep (2026-05-15)\\n\\n### A) 审稿 Warning 待修复清单（Paper Agent 负责）\\n\\n| # | Issue ID | 要改什么 | Paper Section | 优先级 |\\n|---|----------|---------|---------------|--------|\\n| 1 | W3 | 添加 LoRA (Hu et al., 2022) 引用——DCOL v4/v5 用了 LoRA 微调 | Related Work / Method (DCOL 描述) | Medium |\\n| 2 | W5 | 论文文本中确认数据生成使用无条件生成（unconditional generation），实验数据已验证 ✅ | §3 Benchmark Construction | Medium |\\n| 3 | W6 | 纳入 per-class recall 表（cross-model LOO depth_2 recall 低至 26.8%，cross-strategy d2 recall 3.7-19%），不能只报 mean accuracy | §5 Results / Generalization subsection | High |\\n| 4 | R2-W2 | 术语一致性审查：统一 \\\"generation depth\\\" vs \\\"recursive depth\\\" vs \\\"iteration depth\\\" 等变体 | 全文 | Medium |\\n| 5 | R2-W4 | Limitations section 位置调整（当前可能在 Discussion 内，需按 ARR 格式独立成节或按审稿建议调整） | Limitations | Low |\\n| 6 | R2-W6 | 清理 orphan bib entries（bib 文件中存在未被正文引用的条目） | Bibliography | Low |\\n\\n### B) Critical 待修复清单\\n\\n| # | Issue ID | 要改什么 | Paper Section | 依赖 |\\n|---|----------|---------|---------------|------|\\n| 1 | R1-N1 | EDD 正式数学定义：给出公式 EDD(T) = max{d : AUC(d, d+1) ≥ T}，含 threshold T 说明 | §4 Depth Degradation Atlas / EDD 定义 | 无 |\\n| 2 | R1-N3 | 添加 end-to-end finetune baseline（DeBERTa/RoBERTa）对比表，证明手工特征+简单分类器优于端到端方法 | §5 Results / Baselines | ✅ N2 完成 |\\n| 3 | R2-N2 | Bibliography 扩充至 25+ refs，覆盖 watermarking / model collapse theory / data curation / binary detectors / decoding effects（具体列表见 paper_framing.md Dream D002 §3） | Bibliography | 无 |\\n\\n### C) 决策驱动的论文修改\\n\\n| # | Decision | 论文修改内容 | 影响 Section |\\n|---|----------|-------------|-------------|\\n| 1 | D014/D021 | **删除 z-score 恢复 LR cross-strategy d1v2 AUC 的 claim**。实验证伪：z-score 对 LR temp09 holdout d1v2 AUC 无效甚至更差（GPT-2 XL 0.50→0.24, OLMo 0.50→0.19）。保留 z-score 提升整体 LR acc（25%→61%）但明确标注 pairwise d1v2 不恢复。结论改为\\\"genuine structural distributional shift, not correctable scale artifact\\\" | §6 Discussion (cross-strategy analysis) |\\n| 2 | D018 | **DCOL 降级为 negative analysis section**。6 次迭代全部失败（2 backbone × 多种超参），端到端方法 acc=64.5% << RF 78.2%。写 ~0.5 page \\\"Why contrastive ordinal learning fails for depth recursive_gen_depth_est\\n\\n> 非 metrics 性质的决策原因、约束、经验教训。Metrics 查 registry。\\n\\n## 决策原因\\n\\n- **CORAL 需要充分训练**: epochs=5 只得 53.7%，100 epochs 后 78.1%。d1v2 AUC 从 0.34→0.82。之前\\\"CORAL 不适合此任务\\\"的结论是训练不足导致的误判。(2026-05-14)\\n- **CE-MLP 可能也训练不足**: baseline battery 首次跑 CE-MLP 用了 5 epochs 得 74.9%，需要用默认参数重跑确认。(2026-05-14)\\n- **解码策略影响是模型依赖的 (D006, updated)**: Pythia temp09 降 1.9pp，但 GPT-2 XL temp09 升 2.1pp，OLMo temp09 升 3.26pp。论文不能说\\\"strategy-agnostic\\\"，应说\\\"strategy-dependent magnitude with consistent signal presence\\\"。(2026-05-15)\\n- **OLMo temp09 是当前最强配置 (D008)**: 82.03% 4-class RF, d1v2 AUC 0.901。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Feature importance 表述 (D007)**: top feature set (p90_ppl, var_surprisal) 跨 cells 稳定，但 ranking 因模型而异。Claim 2 应说\\\"consistent feature set\\\"而非\\\"var_surprisal as single best\\\"。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Top-k 信号衰减**: Pythia top-k 69.6% vs nucleus 78.2% (-8.6pp)，截断式采样比连续概率重分配保留更弱的深度信号。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Atlas v2 EDD 差异 (D009)**: 6/7 cells EDD=3，但 Pythia topk50 EDD=1（d2v3 AUC=0.692）。\\\"小模型+受限采样→退化信号更早饱和\\\"是 Discussion 核心论点。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Atlas v2 Feature trajectory (D010)**: p90_ppl/var_surprisal/mean_surprisal 随 depth 单调递减（ρ=-1.0），rep_2gram/3gram/4gram 随 depth 单调递增。两类信号互补。(2026-05-15)\\n- **EDD 推荐阈值 T=0.75 (D011)**: T=0.75 下 7/9 维持 EDD=3，仅 topk 掉队。瓶颈统一在 d2v3 pair。(2026-05-15)\\n- **topk 跨模型一致性最高 (D012)**: ρ=0.811，确定性截断使 feature landscape 趋同，但伴随信号衰减。(2026-05-15)\\n- **p90_ppl 优于 mean_ppl 的统计根因**: 1 个样本 (mean_ppl=1.43e13) 贡献 depth-1 mean_ppl 的 94.4%。Token-level perplexity 极度重尾（kurtosis>4900）。p90_ppl 完美单调。论文应说明 percentile-based features 的统计稳健性优势。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Cross-strategy LOO temp09 d1v2 反转根因 (D014, updated 2026-05-15)**: Covariate shift 确认。temp09 depth N ≈ 其他策略 depth N+1。**⚠️ 重要修正：z-score 修复效果取决于分类器类型且实验验证与 framing 声称不符**。RF 对 z-score 完全不变（树模型 scale-invariant）。LR cross-strategy z-score 实验 (lr_zscore_cross_strategy_001) 结果：GPT-2 XL temp09 holdout d1v2 AUC 0.50→0.24（framing 声称 0.13→0.68），OLMo 0.50→0.19（framing 声称 0.25→0.83）。**z-score 对 LR temp09 holdout d1v2 AUC 无效甚至更差**，说明偏移不仅是 scale 问题。论文必须删除/修改 z-score 恢复 LR AUC 的 claim。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Cross-model LOO 3-class (plan_005 detailed)**: mean acc 55.4%，d2 recall 最低 (0.409)——d2 夹在 d1/d3 之间最难区分，符合 ordinal structure 预期。(2026-05-15)\\n- **6-class pairwise AUC 不可与 4-class 直比**: 决策边界稀释导致系统性偏低（d2v3 从 4-class 0.80 降到 0.69）。EDD 判定必须用 dedicated binary pairwise classifier。(2026-05-15)\\n- **EDD=4 (Pythia nucleus, binary pairwise 验证)**: d3v4 AUC=0.784 (>0.75 ✅), d4v5 AUC=0.683 (<0.75 ❌)。深度信号在 d4 仍可区分，d5 饱和。Binary classifier 一致优于 4-class pipeline（d1v2: 0.925 vs 0.861, d2v3: 0.823 vs 0.801），确认决策边界稀释效应。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Granularity ablation 3-class < 4-class 是结构性的，非异常 (D019)**: 3-class 排除 d0（real text），所以 accuracy 低于 4-class。含 d0 的粒度趋势：binary 99.5% > 4-class 78.3% > 6-class 62.9%，严格单调下降。(2026-05-15)\\n- **Boundary dilution 量化 (D020)**: d2v3 AUC 从 4-class 0.804 → 6-class 0.689（-14.3%），d0v1 AUC reviewer concern \\\"也许换个 PLM 就能追上 RF\\\"。两种架构（MLM vs disentangled attention）得到相同结论 = architecture-independent finding。论文可以用 \\\"architecture-independent\\\" 措辞。\\n- **PLM 表征 vs 手工特征互补性 (2026-05-15)**: DeBERTa feature overlap analysis: Spearman ρ=-0.484 (p=0.036), top-5 feature overlap=0。PLM 擅长编码表面特征 (TTR R²=0.85, rep_2gram R²=0.82)，而 RF 依赖的 perplexity 分布统计量 (var_surprisal, p90_ppl) {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — recursive_gen_depth_est\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR May 25, 2026)\\n- Backup: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, full paper August 1, 2026)\\n- EMNLP 2026 special theme \\\"New Missions for NLP Research\\\" explicitly calls for work on data contamination, synthetic data feedback loops, and data governance — direct venue fit\\n\\n## Core Claims (2 条 + Discussion implication)\\n\\n1. **Ordinal depth estimation is feasible and accurate**: Given text alone, recursive generation depth (d ∈ {0,1,2,...,K}) can be estimated with high accuracy using statistical features from reference-model perplexity distributions. On our 3-model × 3-strategy benchmark, Random Forest classifiers on 19 statistical features achieve 70–85% four-class accuracy (chance=25%), with the best configuration (GPT-2 XL, temp τ=0.9) reaching 85.4%. Feature-based methods (RF, CORAL, DivEye) consistently outperform single-feature baselines across all 9 settings.\\n\\n2. **Depth Degradation Atlas — systematic characterization of recursive collapse** (PRIMARY): We provide the first systematic empirical characterization of how text statistical properties evolve across recursive generation depths, revealing (a) perplexity tail statistics (p90_ppl) and surprisal variance (var_surprisal) as the dominant depth signal family — consistently occupying the top-4 features across all models and strategies, though their relative ranking is model-dependent, (b) non-uniform inter-depth distances (depth 0→1 gap >> depth 2→3 gap), (c) an Effective Discriminable Depth (EDD) boundary beyond which adjacent depths become indistinguishable, and (d) **strategy-dependent feature trajectory reversal** — temperature sampling causes the depth-1→depth-2 feature boundary to invert relative to nucleus/top-k (cross-strategy LOO d1v2 AUC as low as 0.13), demonstrating that decoding strategy fundamentally shapes the degradation trajectory rather than merely attenuating it, and (e) **accelerated convergence mechanism** — root cause analysis confirms a genuine structural distributional shift: temperature's uniform logit scaling (vs nucleus/top-k truncation) accelerates convergence to the attractor distribution, so temp09 depth N features ≈ nucleus/topk50 depth N+1 (GPT-2 XL temp09 d1 p90_ppl median=92.2, falls into nucleus/topk50 d2 range 65–92). This is not a simple scale artifact fixable by normalization — z-score normalization improves overall LR cross-strategy accuracy (25%→61%) but does **not** recover pairwise d1v2 AUC (GPT-2 XL 0.50→0.24, OLMo 0.50→0.19), and does **not** improve tree-based classifiers (RF Δ<0.02pp) — confirming a genuine geometry-level shift in feature space rather than a correctable scale difference. This atlas spans 3 model families × 3 decoding strategies.\\n\\n3. **[Discussion — Practical Implication]** Depth-graduated curation as alternative to binary filtering: predicted depth provides a finer-grained quality proxy than binary real/synthetic labels, enabling graduated weighting strategies. Conceptual argument supported by depth-accuracy correlation in 3×3 matrix data; full experimental validation deferred to future work (D012).\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: Web-scraped training corpora increasingly contain multi-generational synthetic content from uncontrolled model→data→model feedback loops. Existing detectors answer \\\"is this synthetic?\\\" but not \\\"how many recursive cycles has this undergone?\\\" — a critical distinction since 1st-gen synthetic data can improve training while deeply recursive text drives distribution collapse.\\n\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Binary detectors (LOKI, DivEye, Binoculars) classify real vs. synthetic but discard ordinal depth information. EditLens estimates single-pass AI editing extent but not recursive depth. Model collapse studies (Drayson et al. EMNLP 2025; Shumailov et al. Nature 2024) characterize degradation patterns but assume depth is known a priori — no method estimates depth from text alone.\\n\\n- **Our framing**: We formalize ordinal generation depth estimation as a new task, construct a multi-generational benchmark (3 models × 3 strategies × 4 depths), and provide the first **Depth Degradation Atlas** — a systematic empirical map of how text statistical properties evolve across recursive depths, models, and strategies. The Atlas reveals (a) perplexity tail statistics as universal depth markers across all 9 settings, (b) model-dependent strategy interaction effects (temperature enhances signal for some models, attenuates for others), and (c) the Effective Discriminable Depth (EDD) boundary beyond which adjacent depths become indistinguishable. We show that feature-based classifiers achieve 70–85% four-class accuracy, demonstrating the task is non-trivial but solvable with the right feature family.\\n\\n- **Headline result template**: \\\"On our multi-generational benchmark spanning 3 model families and 3 decoding strategies, feature-based ordinal depth estimation achieves 70–85% accuracy (chance=25%), with perplexity tail statistics (p90_ppl, var_surprisal) consistently among the top-4 features across all 9 settings. The Depth Degradation Atlas reveals that effective discriminable depth extends to depth 3 in 7/9 settings (T=0.75), with model-strategy interaction effects: temperature sampling enhances signal for GPT-2 XL (+2.1pp) but attenuates it for Pythia (−1.9pp).\\\"\\n- **Note**: 4-class (depth 0-3) is the confirmed setting. 6-class (depth 0-5) pending plan_011 results.\\n\\n## DCOL Status — Contingency Activated (D010/D011, 2026-05-15)\\n\\nDCOL v1–v5 全部失败（embedding collapse / OOM / catastrophic forgetting / 超参违规 / NaN 梯度爆炸）。\\n\\n- **Claim 2 (Atlas/EDD) 升为 PRIMARY contribution**\\n- **Claim 1**: Feature-based methods (RF/CORAL/DivEye) 作为 depth estimation 方法，不依赖 DCOL\\n- **DCOL v6 (RoBERTa-large)**: 非阻塞尝试。成功 → Section 5 bonus；失败 → negative analysis section（\\\"contrastive ordinal learning 在文本深度估计的固有局限\\\"）\\n- **Paper type**: Task formalization + systematic empirical study + practical framework（非 method paper）\\n- At\\n\\n...
